+                // snapshots, so scheduling never changes the sampled rays.
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(i, rowRng) shared(state, completed)

+                    {
+                        ++completed;

+                    }

// ============================================================================
// Precomputation Phase
// ============================================================================

void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    std::vector<size_t> sampleCounts(n);
    size_t completed = 0;

    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(workerCount(n))
    {
        // Culled pairs consume no random values; every other pair consumes
        // four barycentric samples for each ray, regardless of visibility.
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            size_t pairs = 0;
            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                if (i != j && !(state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) > 0.99f)) {
                    ++pairs;
                }
            }
            sampleCounts[i] = pairs * (4 * NUM_RAYS);
        }

        #pragma omp single
        {
            RandomGenerator rng(42);
            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                RandomGenerator rowRng = rng;
                // Only the producer advances the seeded stream. Tasks own
                // snapshots, so scheduling never changes the sampled rays.
                #pragma omp task firstprivate(i, rowRng) shared(state, completed)
                {
                    for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
                        if (i == j) continue;
                        state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
                            i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rowRng);
                    }
                    #pragma omp critical(roomsim_form_progress)
                    {
                        ++completed;
                        if (completed % 100 == 0 || completed == state.numTriangles) {
                            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", completed, state.numTriangles);
                        }
                    }
                }
                rng.discardSamples(sampleCounts[i]);
            }
        }
    }
}

void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");

    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    std::vector<Vec3> centers(n);
    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(workerCount(n * n, 16384))
    {
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            centers[i] = state.triangles[i].center();
        }
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                if (i == j) continue;
                val_t dist = (centers[i] - centers[j]).norm();
                state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
            }
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
// ============================================================================

void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");

    // All off-diagonal delays are positive for this mesh. Each receiver reads
    // completed timesteps only; the worksharing barrier publishes the new row.
    // Keep one team alive and preserve the original order of each float sum.
    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(workerCount(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 16384))
    {
    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
            val_t sumB = ZERO;

            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
                if (i == j) continue;

                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];

                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;

                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];
                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;

                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];
                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;

                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
            }

            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];
        }

        #pragma omp master
        {
            if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
                printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
            }
        }
    }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
// ============================================================================

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");

    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(workerCount(state.numTriangles * state.numTimesteps * state.numTimesteps / 2, 32768))
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
        int bestT = 0;

        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;

            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];
                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];
                sum += pS * pB;
            }

            if (sum > maxCorr) {
                maxCorr = sum;
                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
            }
        }

        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Validation
// ============================================================================

bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
    printf("\nValidation:\n");

    // Check that distances are non-negative
    bool allNonNegative = true;
    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
    val_t sumDist = ZERO;
    int nonZeroCount = 0;

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t d = state.distances[i];
        if (d < 0) {
            allNonNegative = false;
            printf("  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
        }
        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {
            printf("  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
            return false;
        }
        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
        sumDist += d;
        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;
    }

+// Amortize team/barrier overhead for small workloads, while respecting the
+// thread limit selected through the OpenMP runtime.
+int workerCount(size_t workItems, size_t itemsPerThread = 1) {
+    size_t usefulThreads = std::max(size_t{1}, workItems / itemsPerThread);
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), usefulThreads));
+}

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;

+// Amortize team/barrier overhead for small workloads, while respecting the
+// thread limit selected through the OpenMP runtime.
+int workerCount(size_t workItems, size_t itemsPerThread = 1) {
+    size_t usefulThreads = std::max(size_t{1}, workItems / itemsPerThread);
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), usefulThreads));
+}

+    void discardSamples(size_t count) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) (void)rand();

+// Amortize team/barrier overhead for small workloads, while respecting the
+// thread limit selected through the OpenMP runtime.
+int workerCount(size_t workItems, size_t itemsPerThread = 1) {

The larger 1,280-triangle case also matches every checked array bit for bit at 32, 64, 128, and 256 threads. It runs in about 0.26 seconds at 128 threads, compared with about 27 seconds sequentially. I’m rebuilding the final version and confirming that CMake requires OpenMP.
exec
/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import os, pathlib, subprocess, re
root=pathlib.Path.cwd()
work=pathlib.Path('/tmp/roomsim-verify-lgm8wxgv')
subprocess.run(['cmake','--build',str(work/'build'),'-j','4'],check=True)
harness=r'''
#undef main
int main(int, char** argv) {
    SimulationState state;
    initializeSimulation(state,getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(std::atoi(argv[1])),std::strtoull(argv[2],nullptr,10),std::strtoull(argv[3],nullptr,10),std::strtof(argv[4],nullptr));
    computeTimeDelays(state); computeFormFactors(state); runSimulation(state); computeDistances(state);
    auto fingerprint=[](const auto& data,const char* name) {
        hash h; for(const auto& value:data) h.add(value);
        printf(\"CHECK %s %016llx\\n\",name,static_cast<unsigned long long>(h.get()));
    };
    fingerprint(state.areas,\"areas\"); fingerprint(state.kij,\"kij\"); fingerprint(state.tau,\"tau\");
    fingerprint(state.radE,\"radE\"); fingerprint(state.radB,\"radB\"); fingerprint(state.distances,\"distances\");
}
'''
source='#define main roomsim_original_main\\n'+(root/'roomsim/roomsim.cpp').read_text()+harness
subprocess.run(['g++','-std=c++20','-O3','-march=native','-Wall','-Wextra','-Wpedantic','-fopenmp','-x','c++','-','-I',str(root/'roomsim'),'-o',str(work/'parallel-check'),'-lm'],input=source,text=True,check=True)
expected=['CHECK areas a422407a5067d2f5','CHECK kij 240d1b58fce89d8f','CHECK tau 8f4e6f646a91f945','CHECK radE 566d270aa981d225','CHECK radB f8e49468a3f30f09','CHECK distances 33108e3a82d9ce75']
for extra in [dict(OMP_NUM_THREADS='64',OMP_DYNAMIC='FALSE'),dict(OMP_NUM_THREADS='16',OMP_DYNAMIC='TRUE'),dict(OMP_NUM_THREADS='8',OMP_DYNAMIC='FALSE',OMP_THREAD_LIMIT='3')]:
    env=dict(os.environ,OMP_PROC_BIND='close',OMP_PLACES='cores',**extra)
    run=subprocess.run([str(work/'parallel-check'),'1280','100','1199','0.95'],env=env,text=True,capture_output=True,check=True)
    actual=[x for x in run.stdout.splitlines() if x.startswith('CHECK ')]
    assert actual==expected,(extra,expected,actual)
    print('Final six-array exact comparison passed:',extra,flush=True)
ref=subprocess.run([str(work/'baseline'),'-n','320','-t','50','-v','-o'],text=True,capture_output=True,check=True)
run=subprocess.run([str(work/'build/roomsim'),'-n','320','-t','50','-v','-o'],text=True,capture_output=True,check=True)
for expression in [r'=== RESULTS ===.*?=== END RESULTS ===',r'Validation:\\n.*',r'Result hash: \\w+']:
    assert re.search(expression,ref.stdout,re.S).group()==re.search(expression,run.stdout,re.S).group(),expression
print('Final default OpenMP environment: output and validation match the original',flush=True)

a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\\n        u = 1.0f - u;\\n        v = 1.0f - v;\\n    }\\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\\n                            const Vec3& v0, const ============================================================================\\n\\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\\n                  const Octree& octree,\\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\\n    val_t == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\\n\\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\\n        }\\n        return false;\\n    });\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\\n}\\n\\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\\n                  const Octree& octree,\\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\\n\\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\\n\\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\\n\\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\\n\\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\\n\\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\\n\\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\\n\\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\\n\\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\\n    }\\n\\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\\n}\\n\\n// ============================================================================\\n// Room Response Simulation State\\n// ============================================================================\\n\\nstruct SimulationState {\\n    size_t numTriangles;\\n    size_t numTimesteps;\\n\\n  constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
+
+// Amortize team/barrier overhead for small workloads, while respecting the
+// thread limit selected through the OpenMP runtime.
+int workerCount(size_t workItems, size_t itemsPerThread = 1) {
+    size_t usefulThreads = std::max(size_t{1}, workItems / itemsPerThread);
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), usefulThreads));
+}

+    {
+        // Culled pairs consume no random values; every other pair consumes
+        // four barycentric samples for each ray, regardless of visibility.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

+        {
+            RandomGenerator rng(42);
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                RandomGenerator rowRng = rng;
+                // Only the producer advances the seeded stream. Tasks own
+                // snapshots, so scheduling never changes the sampled rays.
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(i, rowRng) shared(state, completed)
+                {
+                    for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
+                        if (i == j) continue;
+                        state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
+                            i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rowRng);
+                    }
+                    #pragma omp critical(roomsim_form_progress)
+                    {
+                        ++completed;
+                        if (completed % 100 == 0 || completed == state.numTriangles) {
+                            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", completed, state.numTriangles);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                if (i + 1 < n) rng.discardSamples(sampleCounts[i]);
+            }

+// Amortize team/barrier overhead for small workloads, while respecting the
+// thread limit selected through the OpenMP runtime.
+int workerCount(size_t workItems, size_t itemsPerThread = 1) {
+    size_t usefulThreads = std::max(size_t{1}, workItems / itemsPerThread);
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), usefulThreads));
+}

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Amortize team/barrier overhead for small workloads, while respecting the
+// thread limit selected through the OpenMP runtime.
+int workerCount(size_t workItems, size_t itemsPerThread = 1) {
+    size_t usefulThreads = std::max(size_t{1}, workItems / itemsPerThread);
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), usefulThreads));
+}
+
 // ============================================================================

 // ============================================================================

 public:
     explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
     val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
+
+    // Advance through the same distribution as sampling, so copied generators
+    // reproduce the original stream even if the distribution's implementation
+    // consumes more than one engine value per sample.
+    void discardSamples(size_t count) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) (void)rand();
+    }
 };

+    {
+        // Culled pairs consume no random values; every other pair consumes
+        // four barycentric samples for each ray, regardless of visibility.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            size_t pairs = 0;
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+                if (i != j && !(state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) > 0.99f)) {

+            RandomGenerator rng(42);
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+                RandomGenerator rowRng = rng;
+                // Only the producer advances the seeded stream. Tasks own
+                // snapshots, so scheduling never changes the sampled rays.
+                #pragma omp task firstprivate(i, rowRng) shared(state, completed)
+                {
+                    for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
+                        if (i == j) continue;
+                        state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
+                            i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rowRng);
+                    }
+                    #pragma omp critical(roomsim_form_progress)
+                    {
+                        ++completed;
+                        if (completed % 100 == 0 || completed == state.numTriangles) {
+                            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", completed, state.numTriangles);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                if (i + 1 < n) rng.discardSamples(sampleCounts[i]);
+            }
         }
